6240|Welcome to Riftbond Basics featuring the
6243|ground rules. Riftbond is a trading card
6246|game based on the League of Legends
6247|universe in which the aim is to score
6249|eight victory points across two
6250|different battlefields. Points are
6253|scored by moving your own units onto one
6255|of the battlefields and conquering it or
6257|by holding it for more than one turn.
6259|The different car types in Riftbound are
6261|units, gears, and spells. To play these,
6264|you must pay an energy cost, which is
6265|shown in the top left corner of the
6267|card, as well as a power cost. Energy is
6270|obtained by exhausting runes. These come
6273|from the 12 card rune deck from which
6275|the top two cards are played for free at
6277|the beginning of each turn. If you
6279|exhaust your runes to pay energy, you
6281|must also pay a power cost for some
6283|cards. To do this, you simply place one
6285|of your runes bearing the symbol shown
6287|on the card under your rune deck. You
6290|can also use runes that are already
6291|exhausted for this.
6293|The domains of the runes and the cards
6295|you play in your main and sight deck are
6296|determined by your legend. Units that
6299|you play enter the board exhausted. This
6301|means they cannot move until they are
6303|readyed at the start of the next turn.
6305|Just like all other exhausted cards on
6307|your side, you initially play your units
6309|and gears into your base. There they are
6311|protected from combat. Gears can't get
6313|into combat. If you already control a
6315|battlefield, you also have the option to
6317|play your units directly from your hand
6319|onto it. This is where your units might
6321|comes into play. This is the number
6323|shown on the top right hand corner of
6325|the units. Might represents both the
6327|attack power and the defensive stat of
6330|your units. If a player moves units into
6332|an already occupied battlefield, combat
6335|stunts. Here, spells with action and
6338|reaction speed can be activated to deal
6340|damage or modify might. When combat
6341|occurs and no player wishes to activate
6344|any more spells or abilities, the total
6346|might of all units is added together and
6348|distributed amongst the opposing units.
6350|Players may decide for themselves which
6352|opposing unit they wish to damage. Once
6354|combat is over, any damage is healed by
6357|units that were involved and did not
6359|die. The game ends when a player reaches
6361|eight victory points, though this is not
6363|quite so easy to achieve. Whilst every
6365|other point simply requires conquering a
6368|battlefield to earn the eighth point,
6370|you must either have held a battlefield
6372|for one round or you score two points in
6375|the same round. Now you've got all the
6377|information you need to know to start
6379|enjoying a game of Riftbound.

16807|A key component of playing League of
16809|Legends is gathering XP and leveling up.
16811|Now you can do that in Riftbound 2 with
16813|the newest additions in Unleashed. Every
16816|XP you get will add to your level as a
16819|player and will benefit you in different
16821|forms. So far, three legends have been
16823|released that directly benefit from XP
16825|gain. Kha'Zix Void Reva, Poppy, Keeper
16828|of the Hammer, and Master Yi, Wu Jun
16831|Master. Notice that all of these have
16833|the body domain in common. Master Yi
16836|uses XP passively. With higher level, he
16838|finds stronger passive effects. At level
16840|six, your units are permanently boosted,
16842|and at level 11, your units always enter
16845|ready. Of course, in the main deck, most
16847|masteries will rely on the champion
16850|unit, Master Yi, unstoppable as a win
16852|condition. This one gets cheaper the
16854|more XP you have, and at level 16, it
16856|even becomes unargetable. Combined with
16859|Master Y, Wu Master's ability to enter
16861|ready, he can really swing games.
16864|Kha'Zix and Poppy have the unique
16866|ability to gain XP through their own
16867|legend abilities and otherwise use it
16870|like a resource. While Poppy generates
16872|card advantage by spending XP, Kha'Zix
16874|gains utility by either buffing his
16876|units or moving exhausted units away
16878|from their battlefields back to base.
16881|Since all of these three legends have
16882|the body domain in common, there's a
16884|huge variety of body cards that can get
16886|you the XP you need through the hunt
16888|keyword, for example. Hunt cards grant
16891|you an XP whenever you conquer or hold.
16894|So, winning combats for those decks is
16896|of utmost importance. Cards like Gemand
16899|Hunter, Boracious Gromp, and Master Heat
16902|Tempered can be found in all three
16904|decks, but XP can also be splashed into
16906|other decks. Grim Resolve, for example,
16909|is a strong combat trick that gives the
16911|unit free might on action speed. If the
16913|unit wins a combat, it grants you two
16915|XP. This card might find its way into
16918|other decks as well. Fiora players have
16920|already played around with the
16921|Shepherd's Heirloom, which works like a
16923|Dorance Blade, but does not equip for

21352|>> Welcome to Riftbound Basics featuring
21355|actions and reactions. There are three
21358|different types of spells in Riftbounds.
21360|normal spells, which you can use to
21361|start a chain during your own turn from
21363|a neutral game state. Actions, which can
21365|be used in exactly the same way, but can
21367|also be used in combat to start a chain.
21369|And reactions, which have all the
21371|advantages of the other two, but can
21373|also be used to react to triggered
21375|abilities or spells. Reactions are super
21378|fast and can lead to lots of exciting
21380|interactions as they resolve before all
21382|other spells. For example, if you have a
21385|stupify in your hand and your opponent
21387|activates a spell that really bothers
21389|you, you can use stupify as a reaction
21391|and draw a card before your opponent's
21393|spell is resolved. This might allow you
21396|to draw another reaction that saves you
21397|from their situation, like a defy.
21400|Another exciting example is dealing
21402|damage to a unit with a spell before
21404|combat. If you go into combat with a
21407|unit that's already damaged, you should
21409|make sure you win the fight, otherwise
21411|the units will heal after the combat.
21414|Let's say you have dealt six damage to
21415|your opponent's mind splitter with
21417|falling star and you go into combat
21418|against it with your watchful sentry.
21420|Your opponent might want to activate an
21422|action in response such as grim resolve
21424|to generate extra might and win the
21426|combat. You can still respond to that
21428|with a stupify. You reduce the mind
21431|spplitter's might to six and because it
21432|still has six damage counters on it, it
21434|will die before the grim resolve can
21436|save it. Which one is your favorite
21438|reaction card in Rift Bond? Let us know.

21656|>> A key component of playing League of
21657|Legends is gathering XP and leveling up.
21660|Now you can do that in Riftbound 2 with
21662|the newest additions in Unleashed. Every
21665|XP you get will add to your level as a
21668|player and will benefit you in different
21669|forms. So far, three legends have been
21672|released that directly benefit from XP
21674|gain. Ka'ix Void Reva, Poppy, Keeper of
21677|the Hammer, and Master Yi Wu Jun Master.
21680|Notice that all of these have the body
21682|domain in common. Master Yi uses XP
21685|passively. With higher level, he finds
21687|stronger passive effects. At level six,
21689|your units are permanently boosted. And
21691|at level 11, your units always enter
21694|ready. Of course, in the main deck, most
21696|masteries will rely on the champion
21698|unit, Master Yi, unstoppable as a win
21701|condition. This one gets cheaper the
21703|more XP you have, and at level 16, it
21705|even becomes unargetable. Combined with
21707|Master Yu Master's ability to enter
21710|ready, he can really swing games.
21713|Kha'Zix and Poppy have the unique
21714|ability to gain XP through their own
21716|legend abilities and otherwise use it
21718|like a resource. While Poppy generates
21721|card advantage by spending XP, Kha'Zix
21723|gains utility by either buffing his
21725|units or moving exhausted units away
21727|from their battlefields back to base.
21729|Since all of these three legends have
21731|the body domain in common, there's a
21733|huge variety of body cards that can get
21735|you the XP you need through the hunt
21737|keyword, for example. Hunt cards grant
21740|you an XP whenever you conquer or hold.
21743|So, winning combats for those decks is
21745|of utmost importance. Cards like Gemand
21748|Hunter, Boracious Gromp, and Master Heat
21751|Tempered can be found in all three
21753|decks, but XP can also be splashed into
21755|other decks. Grim Resolve, for example,
21758|is a strong combat trick that gives a
21760|unit free might on action speed. If the
21762|unit wins a combat, it grants you two
21764|XP. This card might find its way into
21766|other decks as well. Fiora players have
21769|already played around with the

21772|Dorance Blade, but does not equip for

23065|Welcome to Riftbound Basics featuring
23068|runes. In the Riftbound TCG, your energy
23070|to play cards comes automatically. Each
23073|player brings their own rune deck
23075|consisting of 12 cards. This consists of
23078|runes in the domains of your legend. A
23080|Draven deck, for example, can play chaos
23082|and fury runes. At the start of your
23084|turn, immediately after you have readied
23087|all your cards and received points for
23088|holding a battlefield, you may play the
23090|top two cards from your rune deck. You
23093|need runes to pay the energy and power
23095|costs on your cards. For example, for
23098|Victor Leader, you must pay four energy,
23101|which means exhausting four runes of any
23103|color and then pay a power cost. You do
23106|this by placing a rune of the color
23108|shown under the energy cost at the
23110|bottom of your rune deck. This process
23112|is also called recycling. Some cards
23116|such as seal of insight have no energy
23118|cost but do have a power cost. However,
23120|to ensure your energy isn't lost, you
23123|may float the energy. That is, exhaust
23125|your runes, place them under your rune
23127|deck, and later use the energy gained to
23129|play another card. Consequently, power
23132|costs have no effect on the current turn
23134|in which you play the cards, but can
23136|have a significant impact on subsequent
23138|turns as you are constantly playing with
23140|fewer runes. How do you like your runes
23142|best in Riftbound? Do you prefer them
23144|furious and chaotic, or rather calm and
23146|orderly?
